# Gielinor Go species

All 1,055 species in `species.json`, with the wiki page, the infobox picture, a biome, and an image prompt.

Biomes were assigned from each wiki page's Location table (place names and map coordinates), then checked by
hand. The **primary** biome is the most typical home. The **secondary** is the next most common, or `-`.
Treat the biomes as a best-effort guide, not canon.

The **image prompt** is a short description of the creature for the ComfyUI artwork pipeline
(`gielinor-go-plans/comfyui/run_pipeline.py`). It was written from the wiki intro and the infobox picture, and it
ends with a `Do not draw:` list that the pipeline moves into the negative prompt. Some pictures are abstract (flat
shadows, swarms, floating objects), so those prompts are best guesses. Edit a prompt here and re-run the pipeline for
that species.

## Biome counts (primary)

| Biome | Species |
| --- | --- |
| Cave & Dungeon | 169 |
| Crypt & Haunted | 141 |
| Town & Castle | 134 |
| Otherworldly | 124 |
| Snow & Mountain | 89 |
| Forest & Woodland | 76 |
| Swamp & Jungle | 75 |
| Coast & Sea | 69 |
| Desert | 66 |
| Wilderness | 55 |
| Volcano & Fire | 29 |
| Grassland & Farmland | 28 |

Do not draw: a human face, a rider, flames, bright colours, a cute look. |

Its paws end in long dark charcoal claws, and a very long, thin, bare purple-grey tail arcs high behind it. It crouches on all fours with its head lowered.
